“It is another opportunity for fitness. Coming into games against teams of good quality, it is important we get good fitness. It is a chance to build momentum for the season ahead. We are taking steps in the right direction. Of course, we want more from the performance and the results, but we take learnings and move on.

“All of us, together, we share a common goal. We have confidence to build on. We will take every game as it comes. The atmosphere was amazing. I did not realise Ireland has so many Arsenal fans. We are so grateful for everyone who came to support us today. We wanted to give them a good result and performance. Hopefully next time we can.”

“We always want to win and in the end we could not today. In some ways they deserved it and we could have done better. We did have some good moments though. We had an amazing season last season. I believe in this team and there are still players to come back from the World Cup. I am confident but we can always do better.”

There is the final whistle in Dublin and Betis secure a 3-1 win over Arsenal at Aviva Stadium. Goals from Riquelme, Deossa and Fornals has led Manuel Pellegrini’s side to the victory. All four goals in the game came before the break.

Jesus gets onto the end of a ball in behind, getting to it just before the byline, and delivers a dangerous cross but Natan does well on the stretch to clear.

Nwaneri catches Bernal and if this was a competitive fixture than I think Nwaneri would perhaps have been booked for that challenge.

Arsenal’s title triumph was built on that stout defence and conceding so few but there have been so many self-inflicted wounds here. Since Gyokeres misses that very good chance just a minute into the game, Arsenal have not really carved out many genuine opportunities in front of goal, other than that Tzolis wasted chance. Every time they reach the final third, they are lacking that cutting edge.

“When I see the training and how hungry they are for every training drill, that means everybody is ready to compete at the highest level. When you are a player of Arsenal, you compete for every title that is possible. It is not only about the Premier League, it is about all the competitions we are playing for. I see a clear mindset and everything, that everybody is on the same page to win titles here. I saw it from the first training and I think when you train like that in the game, it makes it even better. I came here ready, with my fitness level and I was happy that I could show it here as well. I want to be myself and to show on the pitch all the passion and desire to win because I really like to win games and to give everything.

“If I give 100% for every game and every duel, I think who does not like this? I think that is the best thing for a football player, and then you will always have the support from the supporters. I am thinking about how to improve and settle as fast as possible in the club, because I have the opportunity to train directly with the team from the beginning of the season, to play pre-season games and then to go to official games. This is a chance to play directly from the beginning and then it is up to me to show the qualities and what I can bring to the team. I am pretty confident I will show it in the next games.”

Arsenal are closing in on the signing of Newcastle and Brazil midfielder Bruno Guimaraes in a deal worth £75m. He has been given permission to undergo a medical today and is set to join Arsenal in the coming days. One midfielder coming into Arsenal is set to see another depart as Dane Christian Norgaard closes in on a move to Everton after just one season at Arsenal.

Our starting XI for #ArsenalRealBetis! 💚🤍 pic.twitter.com/8rRvy9IZCm

🟡 𝗧𝗘𝗔𝗠𝙉𝙀𝙒𝙎 🔵 Unchanged from last time out.Let's keep our good pre-season form going ✊🤝 Presented by @deel pic.twitter.com/S5TKqR9Lv3

Premier League champions Arsenal continue their preparations for the new season as they take on Spanish side Real Betis at the Aviva Stadium in Dublin, with this being the first-ever meeting between the two sides. This is the first time Arsenal have played a pre-season game in Dublin since August 2018, when they drew 1-1 with London rivals Chelsea, with Arsenal winning on penalties.

Arsenal beat Girona 4-1 in their opening game of pre-season, with new signing Christos Tzolis on the scoresheet in his first appearance for the club. After today, Arsenal have three more games, including the Community Shield, before they kick off the defence of their Premier League crown at home against newly-promoted Coventry on opening night of the new season. They are closing in on a deal for Newcastle captain Bruno Guimaraes, who is set to become one of Arsenal’s most expensive signings. Mikel Arteta’s side have been dealt a blow ahead of the season with the news that France centre-back William Saliba will miss an extended period due to a back injury. The likes of Declan Rice, Bukayo Saka and Noni Madueke are still yet to return after their World Cup exploits.

Real Betis, under the management of former Manchester City boss Manuel Pellegrini, finished fifth in La Liga last season and have therefore qualified for this coming season’s Champions League after a 20-year absence from the competition. Pellegrini has led Betis into European competitions for five consecutive seasons, which includes losing the 2025 Europa Conference League final to Chelsea. They have been busy in pre-season action already having played five games up to this point. They have won four of the five, including a 4-0 victory over Lyon, with just one defeat coming against Granada.

They have a number of former Premier League players in their ranks, including Antony, Giovani Lo Celso, Pablo Fornals and former Arsenal man Hector Bellerin. Real Betis have already played a number of friendlies as they build up to the start of their season, which begins on August 21 when they take on Real Sociedad.

#2307. McTominay: “Il Napoli e l’Italia mi hanno dato delle basi importanti. Ringrazio anche mister Conte”

Scott McTominay, centrocampista del Napoli, ha rilasciato un’intervista a Rivista Undici al termine dello scorso mese di agosto. Ecco le sue parole: “Il Napoli e l’Italia mi hanno dato le basi necessarie per potermi concentrare sul mio lavoro. E naturalmente ringrazio anche mister Antonio Conte, che mi ha aiutato moltissimo. Ho avuto un buon rapporto con lui e con tutto il resto del club fin dal primo giorno. Apprezzo il lavoro che fanno qui per aiutarci a raggiungere dei grandi obiettivi. Siamo tutti una famiglia: tutti i coach, i magazzinieri, i fisioterapisti, i medici. Cibo? Quello che avete qui è il migliore del mondo, secondo me. È tutto fresco. È una cosa diversa rispetto a qualsiasi altro Paese. E me ne sono secondo ancora di più quest’estate, quando sono andato negli Stati Uniti. Io non la vedo affatto in questo modo Riposarmi. Voglio il meglio per me e per chi mi sta intorno. Essere una persona brava è la cosa più importante di tutte. Sono un uomo piuttosto semplice. Penso che nella mia carriera io sia riuscito sempre ad esaltarmi nelle grandi partite, nei momenti più importanti. Vivere certe emozioni mi dà una scarica di adrenalina. A me quel tipo di pressione genera entusiasmo. Scudetto? C’era tensione nell’aria, ma se ripenso alla partita… Ancora oggi mi vengono i brividi. Vivere certi momenti ti fa venire voglia di ottenere ancora di più, perché sono quelli che ricordi con più gioia. È stata senza dubbio una delle giornate più belle della mia carriera. Anzi: la più bella in assoluto. Voglio rivivere un’altra notte così. L’etica del lavoro me l’ha trasmesso il mio grande idolo: mio padre. Io credo che tutto debba partire dalla volontà di lavorare su se stessi, dall’approccio giusto. Se non consideri la tua carriera quasi come un’ossessione, non arrivi mai ai massimi livelli. Conta quello che fai ogni giorno quando arrivi al centro sportivo. Conta il modo in cui fai le cose fuori dal campo”.

I Am Naples Testata Giornalistica - aut. #2305. Caso Galliani-FIGC, l'avvocato Pescatore si autosospende dal Coni alla vigilia dell’udienza

Caso Galliani-FIGC, l'avvocato Pescatore si autosospende dal Coni alla vigilia dell’udienza

Installa la nostra App gratuita e accedi più velocemente alle notizie

Mentre navighi nell'app, scorri il dito da sinistra a destra dello schermo per tornare alle pagine precedenti

Ricorso Galliani, legale Figc si autosospende dal Collegio di garanzia

In vista dell'udienza del Tribunale Federale, Valerio Pescatore, il legale scelto dalla Federcalcio nel contenzioso intentato da Adriano Galliani sulla nomina della commissione di verifica delle candidature, si è autosospeso - apprende l'ANSA - dal ruolo di componente del Collegio di garanzia dello sport presso il Coni.

Il TFN deve decidere domani in merito al ricorso di Galliani, candidato con Giovanni Malagò alla presidenza della federcalcio (assemblea elettiva fissata per il 14 dicembre).

#2304. GdS - Italia, una delle ultime a San Siro? Tutto esaurito per cancellare un'abitudine

GdS - Italia, una delle ultime a San Siro? Tutto esaurito per cancellare un'abitudine

Installa la nostra App gratuita e accedi più velocemente alle notizie

Mentre navighi nell'app, scorri il dito da sinistra a destra dello schermo per tornare alle pagine precedenti

Scegli Fcinter1908.it come tuo sito preferito su Google: clicca qui

Sarà il Meazza a fare da cornice anche stasera a Italia-Francia. "San Siro comincia a pensarci: sarà la terzultima? La penultima? Lo stadio più famoso d’Italia è a sei, teorici anni dall’abbattimento e non può sapere per quante volte ospiterà ancora la Nazionale. Ha una certezza, in compenso: farà rumore per lei. Il Meazza questa sera sarà esaurito: 70.000 spettatori, testa più, testa meno. Gli unici spazi vuoti si vedranno nel settore ospiti, che ospiterebbe 5.000 persone e invece sarà occupato soltanto da 1.100 francesi", si interroga la Gazzetta dello Sport.

Si legge ancora sul quotidiano: "Rispetto alle partite di Milan e Inter, ci sarà giusto qualche seggiolino del primo anello arancio liberato per far spazio alle telecamere della televisione francese, che produrrà la partita in autonomia. Il Santo, peraltro, in questo 12 novembre avrebbe una verità storica da ristabilire. L’Italia a San Siro è stata imbattibile per una vita e per decenni abbiamo avuto una certezza: se la federazione sceglieva Milano, Milano rispondeva con un risultato positivo. Poi è accaduto qualcosa. Nel 2017, lo 0-0 con la Svezia nel playoff condannò l’Italia a vedere il Mondiale da casa, per la prima volta nella storia moderna.

Chi c’era, non ha dimenticato: sembrava di vivere una partita maledetta. Nel 2021, contro la Spagna in Nations, arrivò anche la prima sconfitta: 1-2, doppietta di Ferran Torres. Negli ultimi due anni, ci siamo abituati: abbiamo perso a San Siro ancora due volte, sempre in Nations, contro Francia (novembre 2024) e Germania (marzo 2025). Perdere di nuovo coi francesi metterebbe tristezza, anche perché Milano è la città della prima storica partita della Nazionale, un Italia-Francia 6-2 giocato all’Arena nel 1910. Un ricordo così antico che l’Italia, al tempo, non aveva ancora scelto l’azzurro Savoia: giocò in bianco, e non era la seconda maglia".

#2303. Tresoldi è pronto per il salto di qualità? Taecke: "Se anche non lo fosse ancora, è solo una questione di tempo"

Nicolò Tresoldi è il sogno del Milan per il prossimo calciomercato invernale. L'italo-tedesco, da sempre grande titoso rossonero, in questa stagione ha dato continuità alle ottime prestazioni della scorsa togliendosi anche la soddisfazione di segnare una tripletta a San Siro contro l'Inter. Per strapparlo al Club Brugge servirà un importante sforzo economico, ma dal Belgio la sensazione è che il classe 2004 possa restare in Jupiler League almeno fino a giugno. Del futuro di Tresoldi ne ha parlato in esclusiva ai microfoni di MilanNews.it il collega di for 'Het Laatste Nieuws'  Tomas Taecke.

Tripletta a San Siro, 23 gol in 24 partite stagionali. Insomma, i numeri sono dalla sua: ma lei pensa che sia pronto per il salto di qualità?  "Tresoldi ha tutto per competere ai massimi livelli. E anche se oggi non fosse ancora pronto per essere titolare in un grandissimo club, è solo una questione di tempo, considerando la velocità con cui sta crescendo".

In Italia si vede solo il bello, ma mai il lavoro duro che c’è dietro: in cosa deve migliorare Tresoldi?  "È migliorato tantissimo sotto tutti gli aspetti. Doveva lavorare soprattutto sul gioco fuori area e sul pressing, ed è esattamente quello che sta facendo. Dentro l’area è un mostro. Ha imparato anche a segnare da entrambe le parti, mentre prima tendeva molto di più ad andare sul lato sinistro, come faceva quando si allenava da bambino con suo padre".

Lei crede che un trasferimento a gennaio di Tresoldi possa bloccargli la crescita? "Mentalmente è molto forte. Però la domanda, secondo me, ha poco senso, perché non lascerà il club (ride, ndr)".

#2302. Lazio, Fabiani: “A gennaio non parte nessuno”. Poi sorprende su Floriani

Il ds blinda la rosa per gennaio e, tra i protagonisti della Lazio, indica Floriani: “Ci credevamo, ora sta mostrando chi è”.

Lazio, Fabiani: “A gennaio non parte nessuno”. Poi sorprende su Floriani

CLICCA QUI E SEGUI LAZIOPRESS.IT ANCHE SU INSTAGRAM

Nessuno lascerà la Lazio durante il mercato invernale. A garantirlo è il direttore sportivo biancoceleste Angelo Fabiani, che chiude così la porta a possibili partenze a gennaio nonostante la crescita del valore della rosa.

Il lavoro sul mercato e quello svolto da Gattuso hanno infatti aumentato in maniera esponenziale il valore dei giocatori della Lazio capolista della Serie A. Una situazione che potrebbe inevitabilmente attirare l’attenzione di altri club.

Fabiani, però, non sembra avere alcun dubbio sulle intenzioni della società. E quando gli viene chiesto di scegliere, tra Frattesi, Tavares, Taylor, Belahyane, Noslin e Isaksen, il colpo del quale va maggiormente fiero, il ds sorprende indicando un altro nome: Floriani.

Secondo quanto riportato da La Gazzetta dello Sport, il direttore sportivo della Lazio ha affrontato entrambi gli argomenti nel corso dell’intervista concessa al quotidiano.

La crescita della rosa biancoceleste potrebbe aumentare inevitabilmente l’interesse nei confronti dei calciatori della Lazio. Fabiani riconosce quanto sia aumentato il loro valore, ma esclude possibili “scippi” durante la prossima finestra di mercato.

“Che adesso i giocatori valgano molti di più è un dato di fatto. Ma nel mercato invernale non andrà via nessuno, questo lo posso garantire”.

Parole nette da parte del direttore sportivo. La Lazio, dunque, secondo quanto garantito da Fabiani, non perderà nessuno dei suoi giocatori durante il mercato di gennaio.

A Fabiani vengono poi sottoposti diversi nomi: Frattesi, Tavares, Taylor, Belahyane, Noslin e Isaksen. La domanda è semplice: di quale colpo va più fiero?

Il direttore sportivo parte però da un’esperienza precedente alla Lazio: “Di Napoli, che presi al Messina. Voleva smettere, è diventato Re Artù”.

Quando il discorso si sposta specificamente sulla Lazio, la scelta ricade invece su Floriani.

“Come Lazio dico Floriani. Non è un acquisto, ma un ragazzo che abbiamo cresciuto, mandato a giocare con il premio di valorizzazione perché ci credevamo e ora sta mostrando chi è”.

Una risposta significativa, soprattutto considerando i nomi proposti nella domanda. Fabiani non sceglie uno degli acquisti indicati, ma punta su un giocatore cresciuto dalla Lazio e nel quale, come sottolineato dallo stesso direttore sportivo, il club aveva deciso di credere.

#2301. Italia-Francia, focus Inter: quale nerazzurro sarà determinante questa sera?

Italia-Francia, focus Inter: quale nerazzurro sarà determinante questa sera?

Installa la nostra App gratuita e accedi più velocemente alle notizie

Mentre navighi nell'app, scorri il dito da sinistra a destra dello schermo per tornare alle pagine precedenti

La Nations League entra nella fase decisiva e propone una sfida di grande fascino tra Italia e Francia, in programma questa sera alle 20:45 allo stadio Giuseppe Meazza di Milano. Gli Azzurri di Roberto Mancini, secondi nel girone con sette punti, cercano un risultato importante contro i Bleus, capolista a quota dieci, per avvicinarsi alla qualificazione ai quarti di finale. Dopo l'1-1 dell'andata a Saint-Denis, le due nazionali tornano ad affrontarsi in una sfida dal fascino eterno. Una gara particolarmente interessante anche per i tifosi dell'Inter, che ritroveranno sul prato di San Siro diversi protagonisti della formazione nerazzurra, questa volta impegnati con le rispettive nazionali.

Sono infatti quattro i calciatori dell'Inter convocati dall'Italia: Alessandro Bastoni, Federico Dimarco, Nicolò Barella e Francesco Pio Esposito, mentre sul fronte francese figura Andy Diouf. Un incrocio che aggiunge ulteriori motivi di interesse alla partita, con i nerazzurri chiamati a trasferire in nazionale le qualità mostrate nel club. Dalla spinta offensiva di Dimarco agli inserimenti di Barella, passando per la presenza in area di Pio Esposito, non mancano gli spunti per analizzare anche i mercati dedicati alle prestazioni individuali. Nella nostra comparazione quote di Italia-Francia prendiamo in esame i possibili esiti dell'incontro e le valutazioni dei principali operatori, con un'attenzione particolare ai protagonisti nerazzurri e al loro possibile impatto sulla sfida.

Se sei un nuovo utente per confrontare in maniera più completa le quote può essere utile analizzare anche l'eventuale bonus applicato dagli stessi operatori sui nuovi iscritti.

Tra i giocatori dell’Inter da seguire in Italia-Francia c’è sicuramente Francesco Pio Esposito, profilo che può accendere l’attenzione anche sul mercato marcatori. L’attaccante azzurro rappresenta una soluzione interessante per presenza fisica, attacco dell’area e capacità di farsi trovare pronto in zona gol. In una sfida equilibrata e di alto livello come quella contro la Francia, la sua eventuale rete viene considerata un’ipotesi tutt’altro che trascurabile dagli operatori. Il gol di Pio Esposito è quotato 3.45 da StarVegas e Admiral Bet, 3.15 da Vincitu e 3.65 da Eurobet. Un confronto che evidenzia valutazioni piuttosto allineate, con Eurobet che offre la quota più alta tra quelle considerate.

Se sei un nuovo utente per confrontare in maniera più completa le quote può essere utile analizzare anche l'eventuale bonus applicato dagli stessi operatori sui nuovi iscritti.

Sul fronte francese, invece, un occhio particolare va ad Andy Diouf, altro calciatore legato al mondo Inter e osservato speciale per il mercato assist. Le sue caratteristiche di corsa, inserimento e qualità nella giocata verticale possono renderlo un elemento utile per creare superiorità e rifinire l’azione offensiva dei Bleus. In una partita che si annuncia intensa e ricca di duelli a centrocampo, la possibilità che Diouf serva un passaggio decisivo viene presa in considerazione anche nelle quote. L’assist di Diouf viene proposto a 5.25 da StarVegas, Admiral Bet e Vincitu, mentre Eurobet lo quota 4.00. Anche in questo caso emerge una differenza netta tra gli operatori, con tre bookmaker allineati su una valutazione più alta rispetto a Eurobet.

FRANCIA (4-3-3): Maignan; Zaire-Emery, Jacquet, Upamecano, Truffert; Cherki, Da Cunha, Rabiot; Olise, Mbappé, Doué. Ct. Zidane.

#2300. Gomes scores for Aston Villa in defeat by Bayern Munich in Hong Kong

Aston Villa’s tour of the Far East ended in defeat as they lost 2-1 to German champions Bayern Munich in a pre-season friendly at the Kai Tak Sports Park in Hong Kong on Friday.

Bayern took the lead inside the final 10 minutes of the first half, with defender Kim Min-jae heading in from Tom Bischof’s sublime free-kick. Villa goalkeeper Marco Bizot made a string of saves in the second half to keep Bayern’s lead at one goal until former Liverpool forward Luis Díaz smashed in Bayern’s second off the underside of the bar.

Just as the game appeared to be out of reach for Aston Villa, who had struggled to create much going forward for most of the game, Unai Emery’s side showed some life late on. Summer signing João Gomes’s goal from the edge of the box gave them hope before Tammy Abraham nearly brought them back onto level terms, only to be denied by a fine save from 40-year-old Manuel Neuer.

Aston Villa are next in action in six days’ time when they take on Paris St-Germain in the Uefa Super Cup before kicking off their Premier League campaign at Brighton. Bayern have one more friendly to come against RB Leipzig before the DFL-Supercup against Borussia Dortmund. They then kick off the defence of their Bundesliga crown against Stuttgart at home on August 28.

Stepping up in the big moments! 🐐🧤 pic.twitter.com/r14rF39XpR

Unai Emery’s men head home from the Far East to face PSG in the Uefa Super Cup in Salzburg next week and then have one more pre-season friendly against Borussia Monchengladbach before starting their Premier League campaign at Brighton.

Possession: Aston Villa 44%-56% Bayern MunichShots: 8-16Shots on target: 3-8Corners: 3-7

Bizot nearly has a calamitous moment coming out of his penalty area but gets away with it.

The final whistle is blown soon after and Bayern hold on to beat Villa 2-1 in Hong Kong. They had looked comfortable when Diaz put them 2-0 up but Villa kept going until the end.

Victory in the final match of the #AudiFCBTour! 💪🔥 pic.twitter.com/UFhQhme62i

The space opens up for Palhinha to shoot from range but Bizot makes a fairly comfortable save down to his right.

It was all Bayern for 80 minutes of this game but Villa have come to life in the closing stages, with Vincent Kompany’s side now holding on a bit.

When Diaz scored Bayern’s second goal, Aston Villa were out of this game but it has been all Villa since then. They look the more likely to get a goal.

Gomes’ free-kick out on the left is parried behind by Neuer for a Villa corner on the right. The corner comes to nothing.

For 80 minutes, Aston Villa barely looked like scoring but come so close to getting two goals in just a few minutes. Abraham meets a cross from the right and must think he has scored but his header is brilliantly saved by the 40-year-old Neuer.

Maybe this game is not quite done and dusted yet. A cross from the right picks out Gomes on the edge of the box. The Brazilian takes one touch before toe poking past Neuer and into the Bayern net. Gomes meant that finish and Villa have some hope.

Abraham should have done better there. He has the space to drive down Villa’s right and has a decision to make; cross the ball to his left and drive forward himself to shoot. He takes a while to make the decision before his attempted cross is intercepted by Ito.

Boey’s yellow card was his final act of the game as he is replaced by Della Rovere. Boey did seem to be struggling with some cramp.

Bayern receive their second yellow card of the game as Boey is booked for cynically pulling down Lynch.

Cash, Maatsen and Buendia have gone off for Aston Villa.

Double substitution from Bayern as Pavic and Assomo replace Tah and Brown.

A fantastic goal to put the Germans 2-0 up and it is nothing more than they deserve. Ibrahimovic finds Diaz in space on the left and the Colombian drives at Cash. As he enters the Villa box, Diaz cuts inside onto his preferred right foot and smashes a shot towards goal. It appeared to go right over Bizot, who opts not to put a hand up. The ball crashes off the underside of the bar and goes in. It has been all Bayern in this second half and that goal had been coming.

Bayern are knocking on the door and come very close to doubling their lead. Diaz’s shot from the edge of the box looks destined to hit the net with Bizot beaten but fortunately for Villa Abraham is on hand on his own goal line to make the block.

Bizot makes another great save. Bayern win a free-kick out on the right after Brown was fouled. Bischoff opts to shoot instead of crossing and nearly beats Bizot at his near post but the Aston Villa keeper gets across to his left to make the save.

Unai Emery makes a double substitution as Mings and Lynch replace Torres and Guessand. Mings takes the captain’s armband.

That is a fantastic double save from Bizot, first denying Stanisic and then Ibrahimovic. Both of those shots came through bodies so Bizot did a good job of reacting and making the saves with his legs on both occasions.

It is safe to say the Bayern fans out in Hong Kong love Diaz, who is cheered every time he touches the ball.

Vincent Kompany is going to make a triple change as Ito, Diaz and Palhinha come on. Former Liverpool man Diaz received a great ovation from the fans in Hong Kong. Palhinha, who spent last season on loan at Tottenha, has been linked with a move to Aston Villa in this window.

Should Bayern have been awarded a penalty there? A ball is played in behind the Villa defence into the penalty area and Ibrahimovic appeared to be brought down but the referee waves those appeals away. On first viewing, that appeared to be a penalty.

Just before the hour mark, it is time for some changes from Aston Villa. Abraham and Bogarde come on for Madjo and Kamara. This is Abraham’s first appearance of this pre-season.

Good play from Gomes earns Aston Villa a free-kick not far outside the Bayern penalty area out on the left, in a similar position to the free-kick that Bayern scored from in the first half. Buendia clips in a good ball to the back post, where Guessand looks likely to score but instead misses the ball on the stretch. Should Guessand have done better with that? Probably.

Brown was starting in his customary left-back position for Bayern in the first half but is now operating out on the right wing.

Gomes leaves one late on Pavlovic and, had this been a competitive fixture, I suspect that might have been a yellow card.

Tah is brought down by Gomes and Bayern will have a free-kick out on the right not a million miles outside the Villa penalty area. The delivery though is poor and easily cleared away.

Bayern have brought Stanisic on at half-time for Laimer. Aston Villa have not made a change at the break.

Possession: Aston Villa 45%-55% Bayern MunichShots: 4-7Shots on target: 1-2Corners: 1-2

The first half in Hong Kong is brought to an end and Bayern take a 1-0 lead into the break courtesy of Kim’s goal.

There will be one added minute at the end of this first half.

Aston Villa have struggled to create much going forward and have not really had a genuine goal-scoring opportunity in this first half. Their forward line though will look quite different when they kick off the seaosn than it does today.

The German side take the lead in Hong Kong. Bischof’s left-footed delivery from a free-kick out on the left is sublime, whipping it right into the corridor of uncertainty. The quality of delivery means it is quite a simple header for Kim, who makes no mistake with his header into the far corner. Kim might have been offside but obviously there is no VAR in use today. 1-0 Bayern.

That is a waste in the end from Bayern. Boey fires a pass in-field into Laimer, who takes a fantastic first touch on the turn inside the Villa half. He drives through the Villa half and at the penalty area but his pass outside looking for Cardozo is poor and overhit, with the ball rolling out for a goal kick. Laimer will be annoyed at himself having done all the hard work and then failed to execute the easy part.

Bischof eyes one up from range and tries to pick out the top corner. Bizot is routed to his spot and is hoping the shot goes wide. Bischof’s effort is always curling wide, much to Bizot’s relief.

The first booking of the game goes the way of Bayern Munich. Guessand drives forward into the Bayern half and is brought down by a cynical foul from Cardozo. You were half expecting Cardozo to perhaps escape without a booking with it being a pre-season friendly but he does not. It was the right call to book him.

That was lovely from Pavlovic. The German midfielder shows good feet to beat Gomes before getting his shot away from a central position just outside the box. He forces Bizot into a decent save but one he should be making.

Bayern force Villa into an error and win themselves a corner as a result out on the left. It is delivered into a dangerous area at the front post but Villa do a good job of handling it and clearing their lines.

Bayern Munich have made two signings this summer; Brown from Bundesliga rivals Frankfurt and Saibari from PSV. Brown is starting at left-back for Bayern today with Saibari not involved as he closes in on a return from a knock picked up at the World Cup with Morocco. Brown feels like such a Bayern signing, nabbing a good player from a Bundesliga rival.

Aston Villa have made four signings so far this summer but just one is in their starting XI today in the form of Gomes, who joined from Midlands rivals Wolves this summer. Young Swiss midfielder Manzambi has joined from Freiburg but is currently out injured. Garnacho picked up a knock and Cisse, who has arrived from LASK, is not involved for Villa today.

The ball is played in behind the Aston Villa defence to pick out the run of Binder. On the edge of the Villa box, Binder gets the first touch but Lindelof is calm in defence and heads back to his keeper Bizot.

Moments later, Gomes is dispossessed inside his own half and Ibrahimovic has a shot from just outside the box but drags it wide of the far post.

Good work from 17-year-old Madjo down Aston Villa’s left wins them their first corner of the game. Buendia’s inswinger is headed away too easily at the near post. Aston Villa have seen their former set-piece coach Austin MacPhee nabbed this summer by Xabi Alonso’s Chelsea.

The referee is allowing the game to flow and thus not blowing for soft fouls.

The first chance of the game falls to Bayern Munich. Brown’s cross from the left is headed by Maatsen straight to Ibrahimovic on the edge of the box. Ibrahimovic, not any relation to Zlatan, takes the shot on first time but it is blocked by Maatsen, who makes up for that poor header with the block. The ball goes behind for a corner, which comes to nothing.

Interestingly, there are only eight outfield substitutions on the Aston Villa bench for this game, with the likes of Garnacho and McGinn picking up knocks the other day. It appears then that at least a couple of Villa outfield players will have to play the full 90.

Both sides emerge from the tunnel, Aston Villa led out by Matty Cash and Bayern Munich by Manuel Neuer, and we are closing in on kick-off in Hong Kong. A group of delegates shake the hands of both starting XIs.

Chelsea and Juventus played in this stadium the other night and it is safe to say that the pitch does not look in the best state ahead of kick-off today. It looks like it will continue to rip up and the last thing the two managers will want is to pick up any serious injuries.

Currently, before the two sides emerge, the crowd in Hong Kong are being treated to some pre-match dancing and entertainment, which includes people dressed as pandas and sharks dancing. Would the Premier League ever take inspiration and have dancing pandas before kick-off?

Aston Villa: Bizot, Cash, Lindelof, Torres, Maatsen, Kamara, Joao Gomes, Hemmings, Buendia, Guessand, Madjo.Substitutes: Wright, Asemota, Mings, Abraham, Nedejkovic, Bogarde, Rowe, Burrowes, Carroll, Lynch.

Bayern Munich: Neuer, Kim, Tah, Brown, Boey, Laimer, Bischof, Pavlovic, Ibrahimovic, Binder, Cardozo. Substitutes: Ulrich, Podar-Stiube, Urbig, Kimmich, Luis Díaz, Palhinha, Ito, Chávez, Pavic, Bara, Manuba, Stanisic, Della Rovere, Assomo.

The likes of Ollie Watkins, Ezri Konsa and Emi Martinez are yet to return to training after their World Cup exploits and Aston Villa are also without captain John McGinn and new signing Alejandro Garnacho, who picked up knocks in their win over BG Pathum the other day.

Aston Villa have already sold Youri Tielemans and Morgan Rogers this summer, with Lucas Digne on the verge of a move to PSG, but could another starter be on the way out of Villa Park? England defender Ezri Konsa has been linked with moves to the likes of Arsenal and Liverpool but will Unai Emery be able to keep hold of the 28-year-old?

It has been a big summer of change at Aston Villa already and there could be more changes before the end of the window. Left-back Lucas Digne is set to move to PSG and is set to be replaced by Atlético Madrid’s Matteo Ruggeri. Joao Palhinha, who is on the bench for Bayern Munich today, has been linked with a move to Aston Villa.

“Aston Villa are a top side with a very good coach. They have a large squad and will field plenty of talent. They have already played more friendlies than us, but for us it is a good challenge. It is a match in which we will learn a lot about our team’s fitness. It will be a good match for us.”

“It is difficult to improve; after all, it is not just about the trophies we have won, but also about the number of goals and matches we have won. It is possible, but difficult. Right now, I would say our aim is to win the Champions League; there is no doubt about that. And throughout the season, I will be saying that we need to focus on the next match. To achieve that; to win the Bundesliga, the cup and the Champions League, we need the whole squad to be fit and healthy. We need to make good use of them and improve the squad. We have already done that, but hopefully it will help us achieve our goals.”

Aston Villa’s preparations for the new season continue out in Hong Kong as they take on Bundesliga champions Bayern Munich at the Kai Tak Stadium. These sides have met twice before; Aston Villa beat Bayern 1-0 to win the European Cup in 1981/82 before then meeting in the Champions League group stages during the 2024/25 season, with Jhon Duran’s long-range screamer giving Aston Villa a 1-0 win at Villa Park.

After losing two of their first three pre-season friendlies, Aston Villa have won their first two games on Asian soil and this game marks the end of their tour of the Far East. It has been a summer of significant change at Aston Villa thus far with some key members of their squad departing and some new faces arriving. Youri Tielemans has departed after Manchester United triggered his release clause and they then sold Morgan Rogers to Chelsea for a British transfer record £117m. Unai Emery’s side have brought in 20-year-old Swiss midfielder Johan Manzambi, Brazilian midfielder Joao Gomes and Alejandro Garnacho, with the deal for Garnacho being an initial loan. Manzambi is currently on the sidelines due to a knee injury.

Unai Emery’s side were dealt a blow during the World Cup as Belgian midfielder Amadou Onana picked up a serious knee injury. They were though boosted by the return of Boubacar Kamara after seven months out as he scored in the 3-1 friendly victory over BG Parthum. As one midfielder returned from injury in that game, another picked up a fresh injury as John McGinn suffered a knock to his knee. The likes of Ollie Watkins, Ezri Konsa and Emi Martinez are yet to return after their world Cup exploits. They begin their Premier League campaign with an away game on the south coast against Brighton on August 23 but will be involved in the Uefa Super Cup against PSG next week having lifted the Europa League last season.

Bayern Munich head into this season looking to win a third straight Bundesliga title and have won two of their three friendlies, losing once. They have added Moroccan attacker Ismael Saibari from PSV and German left-back Nathaniel Brown from Bundesliga rivals Eintracht Frankfurt to their ranks this summer. The likes of Harry Kane, Dayot Upamecano and Michael Olise are not available as they are still yet to return after World Cup duty but Vincent Kompany has confirmed that Bayern’s German stars are set to return.

After today, Bayern have one more friendly against RB Leipzig before the DFL-Supercup against Borussia Dortmund. They then kick off the defence of their Bundesliga crown against Stuttgart at home on August 28.

#2294. Lazio, Piazza della Libertà cambia volto: conclusa la grande riqualificazione

Conclusa la riqualificazione del giardino: nuovi viali, pavimentazione e un'area sportiva nel luogo dove nacque la Lazio.

Lazio, rinasce Piazza della Libertà: nuovo volto per il luogo dove tutto ebbe inizio

CLICCA QUI E SEGUI LAZIOPRESS.IT ANCHE SU INSTAGRAM

Piazza della Libertà cambia volto e torna a valorizzare uno dei luoghi più significativi della storia della Lazio. Sono terminati gli interventi di riqualificazione e abbellimento del giardino della piazza nella quale, nel 1900, nacque la Società Podistica Lazio.

Oggi, mercoledì 11 novembre, alle ore 13, si è svolto l'incontro tra il Primo Municipio di Roma Capitale e la Fondazione S.S. Lazio 1900 ETS per presentare i lavori conclusi. Nuovi viali, pavimentazione e un'area attrezzata per lo sport restituiscono un volto rinnovato a uno dei salotti più nobili della Città Eterna.

Una giornata dal significato ancora più profondo per il mondo biancoceleste. Proprio l'11 novembre 2007 moriva Gabriele Sandri, tifoso laziale. Per mantenerne vivo il ricordo, la famiglia ha fortemente voluto la nascita della Fondazione Gabriele Sandri, realtà che proprio da Piazza della Libertà ha dato vita a numerose iniziative sociali e benefiche nel nome di Gabriele.

Secondo quanto riportato dalla Fondazione S.S. Lazio 1900 ETS, la conclusione degli interventi rappresenta un nuovo passo nel percorso di valorizzazione di un luogo indissolubilmente legato alla storia biancoceleste.

È proprio da Piazza della Libertà che ebbe inizio un'avventura lunga ormai oltre 126 anni. Una storia che nel tempo ha attraversato campi di gioco, piscine, fiumi e laghi, parquet dei palazzetti, piste degli stadi, cieli e montagne. Ovunque fosse possibile praticare sport, lì è arrivata la Lazio.

I lavori sono stati realizzati attraverso risorse PNRR, nell'ambito del progetto Caput Mundi, gestito dal Ministero del Turismo e la cui attuazione è stata affidata alla Struttura commissariale per il Giubileo 2025. Gli interventi hanno interessato diverse zone del giardino con l'obiettivo di aumentarne fruibilità, accessibilità e qualità urbana.

Sono stati costruiti tre nuovi viali. Il primo collega via Cesi con l'interno del giardino. Il secondo attraversa la zona centrale dell'area in direzione del viale interno già esistente, mentre il terzo conduce verso la Torretta della Fondazione S.S. Lazio 1900 ETS.

È stata inoltre realizzata la pavimentazione dello spazio che circonda la Torretta lungo i tre lati rivolti verso il giardino. Nell'area centrale sono state posizionate anche due lastre di travertino, utilizzate per la copertura e il livellamento dello spazio.

Particolare attenzione è stata riservata allo sport. Sul lato del giardino rivolto verso Ponte Regina Margherita è nata una nuova area attrezzata. Sono state installate una struttura calistenica con barre per le trazioni e panca, collocata su una pavimentazione antitrauma, oltre a una struttura bikefit.

All'incontro hanno preso parte l'assessore municipale Stefano Marin e i rappresentanti della Fondazione S.S. Lazio 1900 ETS.

Grande soddisfazione è stata espressa da Marco Chiarion Casoni, presidente della Fondazione S.S. Lazio 1900 ETS.

«Siamo orgogliosi e felici che Piazza della Libertà, sede della nascita e parte fondamentale della storia della nostra Società, sia stata interessata da un intervento così importante – dichiara Casoni –. La riqualificazione, il rinnovamento degli spazi e la presenza di nuove attrezzature sportive restituiscono alla piazza una maggiore qualità e una nuova capacità di essere vissuta dalla cittadinanza».

Il percorso, tuttavia, non si fermerà con la conclusione di questi lavori.

«Ma il progetto di valorizzazione non finisce qui – aggiunge Casoni –. Insieme al Primo Municipio stiamo lavorando per continuare ad abbellire e valorizzare Piazza della Libertà. Per noi è particolarmente significativo poter contribuire alla cura di un luogo nel quale, 126 anni fa, è iniziata la storia della Lazio. Ringraziamo le istituzioni e l'architetto Romolo Crescenzi per il lavoro svolto e per aver reso possibile questa evoluzione».

Soddisfazione anche da parte del Municipio Roma I Centro. L'assessore Stefano Marin ha sottolineato il valore dell'intervento per la città e per il territorio.

«Ringrazio la Fondazione S.S. Lazio 1900 ETS per la collaborazione e tutti coloro che hanno contribuito alla realizzazione di questi interventi. Piazza della Libertà è un luogo importante per la storia sportiva e per la vita del nostro territorio. Siamo felici di restituire ai cittadini uno spazio più bello, accessibile e vivibile», dichiara Marin.

Una Piazza della Libertà rinnovata, dunque, ma sempre profondamente legata alle proprie radici. Proprio lì dove, 126 anni fa, iniziò la storia della Lazio.

#2292. Joao Pedro double and Caicedo stunner give Chelsea win over AC Milan

Chelsea cruised to victory over Ruben Amorim’s AC Milan in Jakarta, Indonesia, on Saturday. Goals from Moises Caicedo and a double from João Pedro either side of half-time led Xabi Alonso’s side to their second win on their Far East tour.

The game was fairly even in the first half and looked set to head into the break level at 0-0 but Pedro’s header from Moises Caicedo’s corner put Chelsea in front.

The west London side then doubled their advantage seconds into the second half as Pedro scored his second, assisted by Pedro Neto.

Just minutes later, Chelsea had their third courtesy of a well-worked corner routine. Romeo Lavia’s delivery was met by Caicedo on the volley, with the Ecuadorian’s finish with the outside of the boot finding the bottom corner.

MOISÉS CAICEDO THAT IS INCREDIBLE 🤯 pic.twitter.com/C6CEcUG1sE

Chelsea had further chances to extend their lead in a dominant second-half performance but were unable to add to their three goals.

Chelsea conclude their tour of the Far East with a game against Johor Darul Ta’zim on Sunday in Malaysia before returning home.

Their final pre-season game will be against Real Sociedad at Stamford Bridge before beginning their Premier League campaign at Fulham.

AC Milan take on Amorim’s former club Manchester United next Saturday before they kick off their Serie A campaign at Torino on August 23.

Chelsea managed to lure Austin MacPhee to take care of their set-pieces and today Chelsea scored two goals from corners. Here is a snippet of Mike McGrath’s piece on MacPhee’s arrival at Chelsea:

He is among the new breed of coaches who are rock stars in their own right, taking centre-stage since rules were changed so that only one person is allowed in the technical area at a time. For MacPhee to get closer to the players for a set-piece, it will mean Xabi Alonso taking a step back onto the bench.

It has been an incredible rise for MacPhee, 46, who was working on Michael O’Neill’s staff with Northern Ireland 10 years ago this summer, helping them reach the knockout stages of the European Championship.

Chelsea take on Johor Darul Ta’zim in Malaysia tomorrow before returning to the UK. Their final pre-season game will be against Real Sociedad before taking on Fulham on the opening weekend of the new season.

AC Milan will play Manchester United next weekend and then kick off their Serie A campaign against Torino.

Possession: Chelsea 50%-50% AC MilanShots: 18-5Shots on target: 8-0Corners: 7-2

The final whistle is blown in Jakarta and Chelsea have cruised to a 3-0 win over AC Milan. Goals from Caicedo and a double from Pedro either side of half-time have led Xabi Alonso’s side to the victory. They have one more game out in the Far East before returning home to the UK.

What a performance! 👏 pic.twitter.com/GcUZXwXbRj

Ramos is picked out after making a run into the Chelsea box and is denied by Sanchez. However, the offside flag has gone up anyway against Ramos. AC Milan have barely threatened the Chelsea goal in this second half.

A late change for Chelsea as Sarr replaces Fofana.

Chelsea are seeking their fourth goal as Quenda strikes a shot powerfully from just outside the box but it goes narrowly wide. Xabi Alonso’s side have dominated this second half.

Estevao has the chance to score moments after coming on after good work from the Brazilian down Chelsea’s right but is denied by a good save from Torriani.

Chelsea make a triple changes as Estevao, Mudryk and Watson come on. Mudryk made his return after his doping ban against Juventus during the week.

Chelsea hit the frame of the goal once more. Jackson gets the better of Vladimirov down Chelsea’s left and moves into the AC Milan penalty area. Jackson opens up his body as if he is going to curl his shot into the far corner but instead shoots towards the near corner, with his effort coming off the post.

Ricci goes for an audacious strike with the outside of his right foot from outside the box but his effort misses the target.

Palmer was deep in conversation with his boss Alonso during that water break.

Chelsea made a substitution during that break as Anselmino comes on for Hato. Thus far, Chelsea have made three changes in this game and Alonso did confirm before the game that some players should be able to go the full 90 this far into pre-season.

Chelsea hit the post. Quenda receives the ball on the right side of the penalty area and quickly moves the ball onto his right foot. The 19-year-old then smashes a shot towards goal and the ball flies into the outside of the post. Quenda struck that with some venom.

Palmer has a chance to get Chelsea’s fourth from the left side of the box but AC Milan keeper Torriani makes the save down to his left.

AC Milan have offered very little going forward since the break and Sanchez has not been troubled in the Chelsea goal.

AC Milan have a good chance to get a goal back. Musah finds space on the right side of the box and cuts it back towards the penalty spot. He picks out Nkunku but the former Chelsea man wastes the opportunity.

Palestra is found with a cross from the right to the far post. Palestra takes a touch but his shot goes into the side netting.

Ruben Amorim is demanding a free-kick just outside the Chelsea box but the referee does not agree. Replays show that AC Milan probably should have been given the free-kick as it was a foul by Fofana on Ramos. Both players went down but are back to their feet.

Xabi Alonso is going to make his first two changes of the game as Jackson and Quenda replace Welbeck and Neto.

Chelsea have their third and their new set-piece coach Austin MacPhee will be delighted with that one. Lavia plays a flat delivery from a corner out on the left to Caicedo on the edge of the box. Caicedo hits it first time on the volley with the outside of his right foot and picks out the bottom right corner with perfection.

Chelsea go 2-0 up not even a minute into the second half. Neto, on the right side of the box, cuts onto his preferred left foot and crosses to the far post. Pedro gets on the end of it and, despite the Milan keeper getting a touch on the shot, the ball ends up in the far corner.

AC Milan have made a raft of changes at the break, which includes their new record signing Ramos, who has joined from PSG this summer. Former Chelsea man Nkunku is also on. Milan were back out from the dressing rooms a fair bit earlier than Chelsea were. It is rather farcical how long Chelsea took to re-emerge for the second half and they just saunter out as if nothing is up. Why on earth did the officials just allow Chelsea to take over 20 minutes for half-time?

Nearly 25 minutes after the first half concluded, we are finally back under way in Jakarta.

Possession: Chelsea 58%-42% AC MilanShots: 6-4Shots on target: 3-0Corners: 4-2

The first half in Jakarta is brought an end and Chelsea take a 1-0 lead into the break courtesy of Pedro’s goal on the stroke of half-time.

90 seconds into added time at the end of this first half, we have the first goal in Jakarta. Caicedo’s delivery from the right picks out Pedro, who has a free header inside the six-yard box and makes no mistake, picking out the corner. Pedro really should not have been afforded so much space in the six-yard box.

Palestra makes a run in behind the AC Milan defence and is found but De Winter gets back to put it behind for a corner...

Due to the water break, there will be three additional minutes at the end of this first half.

The first booking of the game goes the way of AC Milan as Pavlovic clears out Palestra. A deserved booking.

Neto and Pedro exchange a good one-two down Chelsea’s right to put the former racing down the right wing. Neto then tries to find Welbeck in the centre of the box but it is just behind him.

Then, moments later, Pedro spurns a great chance for Chelsea. Palestra’s cross from the left is just out of Palmer’s reach but falls straight to Pedro, who blazes over from 12 yards out. He was leaning back and should really be hitting the target.

The space opens up for Palmer to take on a shot from range but his effort is blocked by De Winter. Palmer has not managed to have that much of an effect on this game thus far.

Pavlovic drives forward from his defensive position through the Chelsea half. He gets all the way to the Chelsea box before shooting but he looked to lack conviction, firing off target.

We will pause for a drinks break, which unfortunately we became accustomed to during the World Cup. Palmer takes the chance to take a seat on the bench whilst taking on some fluids.

Neto takes a first-time shot from outside the box and looks to curl it into the far corner, forcing Torriani into a decent save diving down to his right.

That is good one-vs-one defending from Achemapong. Estupinan plays the ball into the path of Leao, who drives through the Chelsea half down AC Milan’s left. As Leao approaches the Chelsea box, Leao attempts to push it past Acheampong but the young Chelsea defender times his sliding challenge to perfection and puts it behind for a corner. Sanchez punches it out to the edge of the box, where Comotto takes it on his chest before volleying towards goal. The ball goes behind for another corner, which Chelsea just about scramble away.

Chelsea hit the post. Palestra drives forward for Chelsea down their left before the ball falls to Welbeck on the left side of the box. Welbeck’s effort hits the outside of the post. AC Milan keeper Torriani appeared though to always have it covered.

Chelsea gift the ball away inside their own half and Leao drives forward. The Portuguese forward moves into the Chelsea penalty area before getting his shot away but his effort is blocked.

A big, big chance for Chelsea to take the lead. A long ball is played forward in behind the AC Milan defence and Pedro gets the better of De Winter inside the Milan penalty area. It is then a one-on-one chance with Torriani but Pedro hits it straight at the Milan goalkeeper. Torriani should have come to claim that long ball and then Pedro should probably have buried that opportunity.

Chelsea win their third corner of the game as Palmer’s cross from the right takes a couple of deflections off AC Milan defender. Captain Caicedo has trotted over to take it out on the left. He sends it into a good area but a foul on the AC Milan keeper Torriani is given against Pedro.

Neto clips a free-kick into the AC Milan penalty area, which is chested down by Pedro. The Brazilian holds the ball up well inside the box before winning a corner. Chelsea play it short and Palmer crosses to the back post, where Palestra wins Chelsea another corner, this time out on the left.

Caicedo’s inswinging delivery is headed away at the front post.

Palestra, who has joined Chelsea this summer from Atalanta, is proving his versatility today; he has mostly operated for Chelsea at right-back so far this pre-season but he is starting at left-back in this game.

Camarda is played in behind the Chelsea defence and tries to get the better of Fofana inside the Chelsea penalty area but the ball just runs out of play across the byline before Camarda can square the ball.

We are under way in Jakarta, Indonesia, in Chelsea’s penultimate game over in the Far East. Before the game started, a huge AC Milan tifo was unveiled at one end of the ground.

It is 7pm in Jakarta yet the temperature is still up at 29 degrees with the humidity at nearly 70%. These conditions will be a proper test for both sides.

The teams emerge from the tunnel and we are just moments away from kick-off in Indonesia.

The trophies won by Franco Baresi, who has died at the age of 66, are their own monument to the AC Milan and Italy defender’s greatness. Six times a Serie A champion, five as captain, three European Cup-winning campaigns, 10 other winners’ medals and the distinction of being a member of Italy squads that finished in first, second and third places at World Cup tournaments demonstrate that his career, in a significantly more meritocratic era, stand alongside the giants even if the bias towards attacking virtuosos denied him the Ballon d’Or his talent deserved.

Yet they do not tell us why the grief, so tangible in Milan and beyond today, transcends tribal rivalry. It would be unfair to say that he transformed the art of Italian defending. Gaetano Scirea, the elegant Juventus sweeper who kept the 21-year-old Baresi confined to the bench when Italy won their third World Cup in 1982, had a surplus of natural ability, composure and intelligence. He did not need the raw-elbowed brutality of Claudio Gentile, heir to generations of Italian defenders influenced by the post-war influx of players brought up in the traditions of Argentina and Uruguay.

The English forward joined Chelsea from Borussia Dortmund last summer for just under £50m. Chelsea’s pre-season continues out in the Far East as they take on AC Milan in the Gelora Bung Karno Stadium in Jakarta, Indonesia. Both clubs now have new managers, with Xabi Alonso taking over at Chelsea and former Manchester United boss Ruben Amorim now at the helm at AC Milan. These sides met in pre-season last year, with Chelsea winning 4-1 at Stamford Bridge. At that time, now-Manchester City boss Enzo Maresca was in charge whilst Max Allegri, who is now at Napoli, was leading AC Milan. The last time they met competitively was during the 2022/23 Champions League season, with Chelsea winning both games.

Chelsea lost 1-0 to AC Milan’s Serie A rivals Juventus on Wednesday in Hong Kong, in which Danny Welbeck made his first Chelsea appearance after joining from Brighton in the last week.

“We want to have a great team spirit and that is built on a daily basis with a great culture in the building,” Alonso said in his pre-match press conference. “That helps in the moment you have to compete. You feel better having that togetherness, trust, and the bonds between the players are so important to ultimately building a winning mentality, and a team that can compete anywhere. So the football side is so important, but the mental side is as important. And the strength of the team is the most important. We have been able to train and prepare [during pre-season] and I am very happy with the attitude and personality, we know that it is a very important part of this tour.”

It has been another busy week on the transfer front for Chelsea; not only has Welbeck arrived over the last week but so too have Jordan Henderson and Valentin Barco. Alonso’s men have two more games after this against Johor Darul Ta’zim and Real Sociedad before getting their Premier League campaign under way with a derby against Fulham at Craven Cottage.

AC Milan finished fifth in Serie A last season to earn a spot in the Europa League last season. Their big-money acquisition this summer as been Portuguese striker Goncalo Ramos from PSG. They have played two pre-season friendlies so far, which have both ended in draws; 2-2 against Celtic and then 1-1 against city rivals Inter. They kick off their Serie A campaign at Torino on August 23.

Un periodo ricco di partite, con l'Inter che sarà impegnata in quattro competizioni: si preannuncia un mese intenso per i nerazzurri, un arco temporale di trentuno giorni con nove sfide in programma. Dal rientro per l'ultima pausa delle Nazionali di quest'anno, la squadra di Chivu è attesa da una serie di gare impegnative e affascinanti fino al 22 dicembre, data in cui è in programma l'ultima partita del 2026 nerazzurro.

Sono cinque le partite previste a San Siro, dove i tifosi nerazzurri potranno far sentire il proprio calore e supporto alla squadra di Chivu per gli ultimi impegni del 2026, con la stagione che entra nel vivo. Alla ripresa del campionato, però, il primo impegno sarà la trasferta di Bergamo contro l'Atalanta, valida per la 12ª giornata di Serie A, in programma domenica 22 novembre alle ore 20:45. Tre giorni più tardi sarà di nuovo tempo di Champions League, con l'Inter che ospiterà lo Stoccarda a San Siro nella 5ª giornata di League Phase: appuntamento per mercoledì 25 novembre alle ore 21:00. Dopo quella contro i tedeschi, il Meazza ospiterà due sfide ravvicinate contro il Genoa: i nerazzurri affronteranno i rossoblù in campionato sabato 28 novembre alle ore 20:45, per poi ritrovarli negli ottavi di Coppa Italia mercoledì 1 dicembre alle ore 21:00, con l'Inter che inizierà la difesa del titolo conquistato la scorsa stagione.

Dopo il match di Coppa Italia, la squadra di Chivu sarà attesa da due trasferte consecutive: quella di campionato in casa del Frosinone, in programma sabato 5 dicembre alle ore 18:00 e valida per la 14ª giornata, seguita da quella in Germania contro il Borussia Dortmund, che vedrà i nerazzurri in campo al BVBV Stadion mercoledì 9 dicembre alle ore 21:00 per il 6° turno di League Phase.

Domenica 13 dicembre alle ore 15:00, invece, è previsto l'ultimo match di campionato di quest'anno a San Siro, con l'Inter che affronterà il Torino nella 15ª giornata, seguita dalla trasferta in casa del Lecce in programma venerdì 18 dicembre alle ore 20:45 allo Stadio Via Del Mare. L'ultimo impegno del 2026, invece, metterà in palio il primo trofeo della stagione: martedì 22 dicembre l'Inter affronterà la Lazio nella Supercoppa Italiana, che si giocherà al Meazza alle ore 21:00.

Un ciclo di nove partite in un mese, con un trofeo in palio in un match da giocare davanti al popolo nerazzurro, chiamato a sostenere l'Inter in questo filotto di partite intense per chiudere l'anno nel modo migliore.

#2285. Man Utd impress in draw against PSG but injury worries mount

Michael Carrick said he had to be careful with Mason Mount after the England midfielder handed Manchester United an injury scare in their friendly draw with Paris St Germain.

Mount has had a torrid time with injuries since signing from Chelsea in a £55m-deal three years ago, starting only 25 Premier League games for United in that time.

The 27-year-old has been successfully deployed by Carrick in a central midfield role during pre-season, leading to speculation that United may not make another expected signing in that department.

But Mount played only 18 minutes of Saturday’s 1-1 draw against the European champions in Gothenburg before being replaced by Scotland international Tyler Fletcher.

“He [Mount] got kicked. We just wanted to be careful and look after him,” Carrick, the United head coach, told reporters in Sweden.

“We’ve been lucky with injuries so far, so we wanted to be careful.”

Post-match video footage showed Mount walking freely on to the United bus without the aid of a protective boot or knee support.

Michael Carrick told VG Mason Mount was taken off as a precaution. Looked OK leaving stadium. pic.twitter.com/Uo0fBtgpAl

United went behind within two minutes at the Ullevi Stadium when Liverpool target Ibrahim Mbaye punished slack marking to put PSG ahead.

Bryan Mbeumo fired United level after 32 minutes following good work from Amad Diallo, the Cameroon forward’s third goal in two games after his match-winning double against Atletico Madrid.

United meet Leeds in a Dublin friendly on Wednesday before coming up against AC Milan and their former manager Ruben Amorim next Saturday.

United open their Premier League campaign at Hull on August 22.

“It was good game. PSG is good so we had to keep building fitness. I am happy to have other [players] back and we have to keep going.”

“It’s good for me. I enjoyed it. We need to build fitness, we have a big season ahead.”

“It’s a good game PSG are tough to beat and we got a draw and now think about the next game [against Leeds in Dublin].”

“We have two more games against Leeds a and Milan. We need to have big season and hope to win a trophy.”

All over in Gothenburg and it’s been a good match for Michael Carrick’s team. They looked sharper and quicker than their win over Atletico Madrid last week (what the manager wanted) and things are moving in the right direction. Two things to worry about, however, were the early exits of Mason Mount and Tom Heaton with injuries. They’ll especially hope the Mount knock isn’t anything mid or long term.

Nearly done and it’s been a good run out for United. They’ve looked the better, sharper side and, if anything, deserve to win. In mitigation, this is United fourth pre-season game to PSG’s second...

Fernandes has one thing on his mind when he gets the ball 25 yards out in the centre of the park - a shot. He unleashes the attempt, destined for the bottom-left corner, forcing Longoni, the sub goalkeeper, into a good save.

Two chances for United in quick succession. A lovely ball from the middle of the park by Zirkzee finds Dalot on the right. He chips the ball to the back post where one of the Fletchers (sorry, I am not sure which) heads for goal. His attempt is blocked and the ball, ultimately, falls at the feet of Lacey whose shot is blazed high over the bar.

There have been plenty of good things for Carrick to take from this match. The passing, while not of the standard of a cut-and-thrust Premier League match, has been good for the time of year, and there have been enough flashes of brilliance/class/invention etc to suggest that pre-season is moving in the right direction.

Lovely bit of play by United. A great pass with the outside of the boot from the inside-left channel by Tielemans finds Fernandes who plays a sidefooted shot that goes just wide.

Tielemans’s first bit of brilliance from the Belgian there.

Both sides losing possession a fair bit at the moment. Strong pre-season vibes right now.

Maguire has had a good game. He’s played a number of long passes from the back and looked assured. This time he’s up in the PSG half and decides to have a pop - his shot testing Safanov, who saves well.

Had there been VAR Safanov might have had something to answer for...he may have beaten Zirkzee to the ball but he flew in the air and jurt the United man in the process.

Anwway, there’s no VAR (hurrah, for those of you who consider it a modern-day evil) and it doesn’t matter.

Chance for United to go ahead and it comes from two things. (i) A long, hopeful ball from Fernandes that looks for Zirkzee (ii) Safanov rushing off his line and into no-man’s land. The ball comes loose and Jack Fletcher, instead of having a go at goal, with the keeper on the ground and out of the game, passes to Lacey whose shot is wide.

Tyler Fletcher loses the ball near the United half and it, ultimately, results in a PSG corner. From the set piece United defend well and snuff out all hint of danger.

PSG seeing more of the ball at the moment. They work the ball to the right and Amass brings down Mbaye.

From the resulting free-kick, in the final third, the French side whip in the ball but Mee is up ot the challenge and come to collect well.

Santos, Mbeumo, Shaw, Amad, Lacey are off

Fernandes, Jack Feltcher, Zirkzee and Amass are on.

A raft of changes for PSG sees Marquinhos and João Neves, among others, come on. More like it, in terms of first-team ‘names’, on the pitch for the European Cup champions.

United on top in this half, as they were in the first.

The paces quickens and the game is much better for it. Mbuemo drives toward the PSG box, he cuts inside from the right before unleashing a shot that is bound for the back of the net before a fine one-handed save by Safanov. Credit, too, for Maguire whose long ball from the back found Mbuemo brilliantly. Promising...

Lacey tries to play in Dorgu on the left but he’s asking too much of his team-mate. Wonder when the expected raft of changes will take place?

Slow start to the second half. This is very much pre-season-friendly pace.

Having seen Mount limp off in the first half, there’s more worry at the start of the second and Heaton is force off with what looks like a hamstrung injury. He dashed off his line to clear a ball and in doing so has tweaked something. Mee replaces him.

Back under way Gothenburg - there have been a number of changes and will bring them to you once I’ve worked out who has come on and who they’ve replaced.

A world record that still stands...(this was back in 1995).

And that is it for the first 45 minutes. PSG took the early lead, but from that moment on United have been the better side and deservedly are level through Mbueno’s goal.

There will be one minute of added time at the end of what has been a good first half for Man Utd.

Amad has a go from outside the box forcing the save from Safanov. He’s such a live wire and one of the best in his position in the top flight. Him or Antony? No contest, strange it took United a while to work that one out.

Yet more good work from the underrated Amad. He again closes down well on the right before winning back possession and cutting the ball back for Lacey. To think he was starring for Sunderland in the Championship while Antony was starting for United...

United have been the better side since conceding earlier and, as if to illustrate my point, as I typed that an Mbuemo free-kick is well saved by Safanov. That was heading for the top-right bin.

Great work on the left by Dorgu and Shaw ends with the latter chipping the ball to the back post where Amad is lurking with intent. The Ivorian looks to head the ball home, but before he can get anything on it it looks as though he’s pushed in the back. United players are sure it’s a penalty but, more importantly, the referee doesn’t.

Amad does brilliantly, winning the ball off Koukaba in the final third, from there he scampers toward the box before playing in Mbuemo who takes one touch before firing in a shot into the bottom right corner of the net.

Free-kick for PSG on the right, near the final third. The ball comes in and it’s straight into the arms of Heaton in the United goal.

Kvaratskhelia, not seen much of him so far, runs into the box, but Amad does well to usher him back out.

The match is pedestrian at the moment, but as I type that, Dorgu, yep, him again, drives down the left and puts in a good cross that is well intercepted by Safanov in the PSG goal.

United continue to see more of the ball, Dorgu’s seeing more of it than most. But, no chances really fashioned, still, plenty of time to go.

Worry for United having to sub off Mount. It looks as though he took a knock to a foot and Carrick and co will hope it’s not a long-term injury. Mount, who’s had a good pre-season, has been replaced by Fletcher, son of Darren.

Foul on Mazraoui results in two things. The Mount has to come off, and, United have a free-kick on the edge of the PSG area.

From the set-piece Mbuemo has a go and it’s a regulation save from Safonov in the PSG goal.

Heaven loses possession in the middle of the park and from there it’s like the parting of the Red Sea as PSG have a three-on-one. Mbaye gets the shot in on goal but Andrey Santos covers back brilliantly and does enough to get something on the ball. He’s started both this match and pre-season well.

United are looking half decent on the ball. They’re passing it well and not afraid to switch things around.

A lovely long ball from Maguire finds Dorgo whose lovely first touch takes him into the box before a cut back is intercepted by a PSG man. Promising signs though for Carrick’s men.

United seeing more of the ball at the moment. They’re being patient but cannot fashion an opening and the ball is worked all the way back to Heaton in goal.

Needless to say this is being played at a slowish pace...no blood-and-guts challenges being put in.

Good work down the right sees Amad plays in Shay Lacey who crosses into the box. The ball find shte head of Mbuemo and the ball sails over the bar. Better from United.

United started slowly against Atletico and they’ve done the same here.

Bright start to say the least from the French side. They work the ball left and it’s an easy cross from Dro Fernandez for Mbaye to tap home for the very early lead.

They’re under way in Sweden. United are in their new vibrant red kit and PSG in their blue, red and white kit. Big fan of the French side’s kit, it harks back to the kit they had in the 1990s when the likes of George Weah and David Ginola were entertaining in the capital of France.

Ready to face PSG in our latest pre-season test ✊

Manchester United continue their Sweden sojourn with match against Paris St-Germain in Gothenburg.

Clearly a clash against the back-to-back Champions League winners is a step up from their previous two pre-season friendlies (victories against Rosenborg and Atletico Madrid), but at the same time this is more a chance for players to get miles in the legs and see how new signings and possibly some young guns fit in.

One of those new signings is Youri Tielemans, and the Belgian international is in line to make his debut after his move from Aston Villa. Other team news is that Bruno Fernandes is back and ready to captain the team.

Michael Carrick has also been boosted by the returns of Matheus Cunha, Diogo Dalot and Noussair Mazraoui after their post-World Cup breaks.

Cunha, however, will not play a part in today’s match because of a “minor illness” according to the MUTV website. He is expected to link up with the squad in Ireland, where they face Leeds United at Croke Park, after the match.

This will be Man Utd’s first outing since the 2-1 win over Atletico, a performance and result that pleased Carrick.

“I enjoyed it, I thought there was a lot of good in things that we are looking to improve on,” the United manager said. “Building on what we did for the last seven months, I think there is a lot of good signs for us, it’s never going to be the exact stage we want to be at. But I thought there was really a lot of positives to take out of the game, individual, collectively as a team, senior players looking strong and fit, and the younger players doing really well.

“The spirit and the camaraderie is so important for the team, it’s great to see the boys getting on so naturally and enjoying each other’s company. Collectively we looked strong as a team, which is always the most important thing, and individual performances seeing the young players come on to have an impact.”

#2283. Gabriele Sandri, 19 anni dalla morte del tifoso della Lazio

Il dj romano viaggiava con alcuni amici verso Milano per assistere a Inter-Lazio quando fu colpito durante una sosta.

Gabriele Sandri morì l’11 novembre 2007 a Badia al Pino. Diciannove anni dopo, il giovane tifoso della Lazio continua a essere ricordato sugli spalti, dagli amici, dalla famiglia e dall’intera tifoseria.

CLICCA QUI E SEGUI LAZIOPRESS.IT ANCHE SU INSTAGRAM

Dj romano e grande tifoso biancoceleste, Gabriele era diretto a Milano con alcuni amici per assistere alla partita tra Inter e Lazio. Durante una sosta si verificarono momenti di tensione tra tifosi.

Un agente della Polizia Stradale esplose alcuni colpi di pistola. Uno raggiunse Sandri mentre si trovava all’interno di un’automobile.

Per la morte di Gabriele, Luigi Spaccarotella fu condannato in via definitiva dalla Cassazione il 14 febbraio 2012. La pena fu di nove anni e quattro mesi per omicidio volontario con dolo eventuale.

#2282. Inter Campus, dopo 29 anni cambia tutto: perché l'Inter esce e cosa succederà al progetto

Inter Campus, dopo 29 anni cambia tutto: perché l'Inter esce e cosa succederà al progetto

Installa la nostra App gratuita e accedi più velocemente alle notizie

Mentre navighi nell'app, scorri il dito da sinistra a destra dello schermo per tornare alle pagine precedenti

Inter Campus e l'Inter prendono strade diverse dopo 29 anni di storia comune. Una separazione che riguarda uno dei progetti sociali più importanti e riconosciuti del calcio italiano, nato nel 1997 per volontà di Massimo Moratti e diventato nel tempo un punto di riferimento internazionale.

Dal 21 luglio 2026, infatti, la gestione del progetto è passata alla Fondazione We Are The Campus, sostenuta dalle famiglie Moratti e Tronchetti Provera. Un cambiamento significativo, che segna la fine della gestione diretta da parte del club nerazzurro.

Il club ha infatti modificato la propria struttura dedicata alla solidarietà, trasformando Inter Futura nella Fondazione Inter EF-ETS, un ente filantropico che si occupa di sostenere iniziative sociali attraverso risorse economiche, beni e servizi.

La trasformazione era stata deliberata nel dicembre 2024 e la nuova fondazione è stata iscritta al Registro Unico Nazionale del Terzo Settore nel maggio 2025, come risulta dai documenti ufficiali della società nerazzurra.

Inter Campus, invece, ha storicamente basato la propria attività su una presenza diretta nei territori, attraverso programmi educativi e sportivi destinati ai bambini più vulnerabili.

Carlotta Moratti, che guida We Are The Campus, ha spiegato al Corriere della Sera:

�Il progetto resta intatto, ma cambia la governance e vive parallelo all’Inter».

Anche dal club nerazzurro è arrivata una precisazione sulla prosecuzione del rapporto di collaborazione.

Il nome dell'Inter e le maglie nerazzurre continueranno infatti a essere presenti nelle attività di Inter Campus, sulla base degli accordi di licenza relativi all'utilizzo del marchio.

L'obiettivo resta quello originario: utilizzare il calcio come strumento educativo, favorendo l'inclusione e garantendo ai bambini il diritto al gioco.

A cambiare è dunque la struttura che gestisce l'iniziativa. Dopo quasi trent'anni, saranno le famiglie Moratti e Tronchetti Provera a portare avanti autonomamente il progetto, mentre l'Inter proseguirà il proprio impegno sociale attraverso la sua fondazione.

#2280. McTominay dal ritiro della Scozia: “Prima dei Mondiali ho avuto bisogno di una scarica elettrica al cuore, ora sto bene”

Scott McTominay, centrocampista del Napoli e della Scozia, ha parlato in conferenza stampa in vista dei prossimi impegni della sua Nazionale: “Mondiali? Mancavano due giorni alla partita contro Haiti. Mi ero preparato bene, ero in ottima forma e poi succede una cosa del genere… Ho avuto bisogno di una scarica elettrica al cuore. Mi sono detto: com’è possibile che stia accadendo proprio a me? Però bisogna andare avanti. Il calcio è così e a volte le cose non vanno come vorresti. Ero in perfetta forma, poi mi sono ritrovato in un letto d’ospedale due giorni prima della partita d’esordio ai Mondiali, sul punto di essere addormentato dai medici… Non proprio una situazione ideale. Ora sto bene. Mi sento molto meglio, molto meglio di prima. Era una cosa che andava fatta, nella carriera di un calciatore possono esserci momenti come questi in cui gli eventi ti prendono un po’ alla sprovvista. Ovviamente per le tv e i giornali la situazione sembrava molto peggiore di quanto non fosse. Amici e familiari mi contattavano dopo aver letto le notizie sui giornali, ma quella che ho affrontato a Manchester è stata un’operazione molto semplice. E’ andata molto bene. Ora sono felice di essere tornato a giocare, il calcio è la cosa più importante nella mia vita e lo sarà sempre: voglio tornare, dimostrare ancora una volta il mio valore e assicurarmi di poter raggiungere un livello superiore. L’importante è tornare più forte di prima. Non importa quanto giochi bene una partita, ciò che conta è sempre la partita successiva, recuperare le energie, curare l’alimentazione, il sonno e spingersi oltre i propri limiti”.

I Am Naples Testata Giornalistica - aut. Manca ancora l'annuncio, ma dovrebbe essere questione di giorni: l'Inter e Carlos Augusto hanno trovato l'accordo per il rinnovo del contratto. Il difensore brasiliano firmerà fino al 30 giugno 2031, con annesso aumento di stipendio.
